- **filesystem**: MUST LOAD THIS SKILL before any filesystem task. Use re.sub(r'(?&lt;!\d)\.(?!\d)', '-', name) to handle dots correctly. For directory analysis, depth starts at 1 for the target dir. Use len(content) for file size. ([lec77/skillvm-data](https://github.com/lec77/skillvm-data/tree/HEAD/skill-variants/targeted/bare/qwen3.5-122b/filesystem-1.0.0))
